    ABOUT CGN MINING                                                       關於中廣核礦業
    The Company is incorporated in the Cayman Islands and listed on the    本公司註冊地為開曼群島，為一家在聯交
    main board of the Stock Exchange (stock code: 01164). The principal    所主板上市的公司（股票代號：01164）。
    activities of the Group are the development and trade of natural       本集團主要從事天然鈾資源的開發與貿
    uranium resources used by nuclear energy enterprises, with a view to   易，旨在成為核電站天然鈾資源的主要供
    be one of the top suppliers of natural uranium resources for nuclear   貨商之一。
    power plants.

                                       Management Discussion and Analysis
                                                         管理層討論及分析

BUSINESS REVIEW                                                            業務回顧
The Group is principally engaged in natural uranium investment and         本集團主要從事天然鈾資源投資及貿易。
trading. As at 31 December 2023, the Company (i) held 100% equity          截至2023年12月31日，本公司旗下擁有(i)
interest in Beijing Sino-Kazakh, through which it held 49% of the          北京中哈鈾100%股權，並透過其持有謝
equity interest and off-take rights of products of Semizbay-U; (ii) held   公司49%的股權及產品包銷權；(ii) CGNM
100% equity interest in CGNM UK, through which it held 49% of the          UK 100%股權，並透過其持有奧公司49%
equity interest and off-take rights of products of Ortalyk. In addition,   的股權及產品包銷權。此外，擁有全資子
the Group had a wholly-owned subsidiary, CGN Global and held               公司中廣核國際銷售公司以及Fission公司
12.62% equity interest in Fission.                                         12.62%的股權。


In 2023, the Group achieved revenue of HK$7,363 million and profits        2023年本集團實現營業額7,363百萬港
attributable to owners of the Company of HK$497 million.                   元，本公司擁有人應佔溢利497百萬港
                                                                           元。


Analysis of business environment                                           經營環境分析

The nuclear power market and its development                               核電市場與行業發展

RISK IDENTIFICATION AND MANAGEMENT                                        風險識別及管理

The comprehensive risk management system of the Group functioned          2023年本集團全面風險管理體系運轉正
effectively in 2023 with all risks under monitor and there were no        常，各項風險均處於密切監控中，未發生
significant risk incidents. The completed major work includes:            重大風險事件，完成的主要工作包括：


Firstly, operating a risk management system in a solid manner.            一是紮實運作風險管理體系。梳理公司各
Sorting out the external situation and internal conditions of each        業務外部形勢和內部條件，明確公司風險
business of the Company, and clarifying the environment in which          管理所處環境。組織公司內部以重大風險
the Company’s risk management takes place. Organising in-depth            為核心、重點項目為主線，深入開展全面
comprehensive risk identification within the Company with major           風險識別。結合公司運營實際，梳理公司
risks as the core and key projects as the main line. Combined with        重大風險應對措施，建立重大風險預警指
the actual operation of the Company, sort out the Company’s major         標及預警閾值。在經營過程中，根據風險
risk countermeasures and establish major risk warning indicators          事項進展及時預警，動態調整應對措施，
and warning thresholds. In the course of operation, timely warning is     按月開展風險監控，定期報告風險管理情
issued according to the progress of risk matters, response measures       況，有效控制重大風險事件發生。
are dynamically adjusted, risk monitoring is carried out on a monthly
basis, and risk management is reported on a regular basis, so as to

Secondly, continuing to optimize the specific risk management.            二是持續優化專項風險管理。各專項風險

future. In terms of financial risk, the Company paid close attention to   財務成本。

     管理層討論及分析

     The Company pays close attention to financial market trends, actively    本公司一直密切關注金融市場走向，積極
     considers its responses to internal and external financial risks,        考慮如何應對來自內外部的金融風險，制
     formulates reasonable models and strategies to ensure the safety         定合理的融資模式和策略，確保公司融資
     and economic efficiency of financing, and takes strict management        安全性和經濟性，並採取嚴謹的債務風險
     measures on debt risks to prevent exposure to related risks and to       管理措施防範公司面臨的相關風險，以促
     facilitate the financial health and development of the core business.    進公司的財務健康及核心業務發展。


     Financing model                                                          融資模式

     Given the complex and ever-changing financial market, the Company        在複雜多變的金融市場環境下，本公司一
     has been exploring diverse financing methods and strives to establish    直探索多元化的融資方式，努力建立短、
     a financing model with combination of short-, medium-and long-term       中、長期資金相互搭配、直接融資與間接
     capital, merged direct and indirect financing and multiple financing     融資相結合、多種渠道並舉的融資模式，
     channels to secure stable funds. In the process of debt financing,       為公司提供穩健的資金保障。在債務融資
     the Company has taken a balanced approach between cost and               過程中，本公司始終遵循成本和安全兼
     safety. The Company aims to obtain competitive financing cost but        顧的原則。公司致力追求具有競爭力的融
     does not consider lowest financing cost as the only objective to avoid   資成本，卻並不以最低的融資成本為唯一
     compromising financial safety and the quality of services received.      目標，以免損害融資安全及接受的服務質
                                                                              量。


     For projects with large capital expenditures and sound expected          對於具備良好收益預算的大額資本性開支
     returns, the Company will prudently consider using equity financing to   項目，公司會謹慎地考慮採用股權融資平
     balance risks and enhance Shareholder value.                             衡風險，增進股東價值。

     Code, the responsibilities between the chairman and chief executive     C.2.1條，主席和首席執行官的職責應予
     officer should be separate and should not be performed by the           以區分，且不應由一人同時兼任。於報告
     same individual. During the Reporting Period, Mr. An Junjing was        期內，安軍靖先生同時擔任董事會主席兼
     both the chairman of the Board and the chief executive officer of the   本公司首席執行官。董事會認為，安軍靖
     Company. The Board believes that Mr. An Junjing serving as both the     先生同時擔任董事會主席兼本公司首席執
     chairman of the Board and the chief executive officer of the Company    行官以確保領導及經營的一致性。
     would ensure consistent leadership and operation.

     The Company has inquired each INED the annual confirmation of             本公司向各獨立非執行董事按上市規則
     independence in compliance with Rule 3.13 of the Listing Rules, and       第3.13條要求作出年度獨立性查詢，並已
     has received the annual confirmation of independence from each            收到各獨立非執行董事的年度獨立性確
     INED. After taking into consider of the factors set out in Rule 3.13 of   認函。經考慮上市規則第3.13條所述的因
     the Listing Rules, the Company is of the view that all the INEDs are      素，本公司認為所有獨立非執行董事均為
     independent.                                                              獨立。

Risk management and internal control                                      風險管理與內部控制

Comprehensive risk management                                             全面風險管理

The Company has basically established a relatively comprehensive          本公司已基本建立起較為完善的全面風險
risk management system. With reference to relevant risk                   管理體系，以ISO31000: 2018標準《風險
management requirements of the “Risk Management Guidelines”               管理指南》及企業管治守則有關風險管理
of ISO31000:2018 standard and the Corporate Governance Code,              的要求作為重要參考，對重大風險進行有
the Company effectively identified, evaluated and managed the             效辨認、評估及管理，嚴格落實《全面風
significant risks. It also strictly implemented the Comprehensive         險管理制度》，並將全面風險業務劃分為
Risk Management Policy. Meanwhile, the Company classified and             重大風險和一般風險，進行分類管理，同
managed the overall risk business, namely significant risk and general    時統籌管理項目投資風險等專項風險，建
risk, integrated the management of specific risks such as project         立了相應的風險管理流程。董事會、管理

DIRECTORS’ INDEMNITIES                                                      董事的彌償保證
Pursuant to the Articles of Association, every Director shall be entitled   根據章程細則規定，每位董事就履行其職
to be indemnified out of the assets of the Company against all losses       務或職責或有關其他事情而蒙受或招致任
or liabilities which he may sustain or incur in or about the execution of   何損失或責任，均有權獲得公司以其資產

friendly appliances, etc. At the same time, the Company also             公司亦不定期組織員工參與植樹活動及提

      4.     MATERIAL ACCOUNTING POLICY                                         4.   重大會計政策資料
             INFORMATION
             (a)     Basis of consolidation                                          (a)   綜合基準

                     The consolidated financial statements comprise the                    綜合財務報表包括本集團
                     financial statements of the Group. Inter-company                      的財務報表。集團內公司
                     transactions and balances between group companies                     間所有交易、結存及未變
                     together with unrealised profits are eliminated in full               現溢利已在編製綜合財務
                     in preparing the consolidated financial statements.                   報表時悉數抵銷。除非交
                     Unrealised losses are also eliminated unless the                      易提供證據顯示已轉讓資
                     transaction provides evidence of impairment on the                    產有所減值，否則未變現
                     asset transferred, in which case the loss is recognised               虧損亦會撤銷，在此情況
                     in profit or loss.                                                    下，虧損於損益確認。


             (b)     Subsidiaries                                                    (b)   附屬公司

                     A subsidiary is an investee over which the Company                    附屬公司指本公司能對其
                     is able to exercise control. The Company controls                     行使控制權之被投資方。
                     an investee if all three of the following elements are                倘達成以下三項條件，本
                     present: (i) power over the investee, (ii) exposure, or               公司取得被投資方之控制
                     rights, to variable returns from the investee, and (iii)              權：(i)有權控制投資對象，
                     the ability to use its power to affect those variable                 (ii)對來自投資對象之浮動回
                     returns. Control is reassessed whenever facts and                     報享有承擔或權利，以及
                                                                                           (iii)能運用對投資對象之權
                     circumstances indicate that there may be a change in
                                                                                           力以影響其所得回報。倘
                     any of these elements of control.
                                                                                           有事實及情況顯示任何有

           (iv)     Effective interest method                                           (iv)   實際利率法

                    The effective interest method is a method of                               實際利率法為計算
                    calculating the amortised cost of a financial                              金融資產或金融負
                    asset or financial liability and of allocating                             債的攤銷成本以及
                    interest income or interest expense over the                               於相關期間分配利
                    relevant period. The effective interest rate                               息收入或利息支出
                    is the rate that exactly discounts estimated                               的方法。實際利率
                    future cash receipts or payments through the                               乃為在金融資產或
                    expected life of the financial asset or liability, or                      負債的預計年期或
                    where appropriate, a shorter period.                                       （如 適 用）較 短 期 間
                                                                                               內能夠精確地將估
                                                                                               計未來現金收款或
                                                                                               付款貼現的利率。


           (v)      Equity instruments                                                  (v)    權益工具

                    Equity instruments issued by the Group are                                 本集團所發行權益
                    recorded at the proceeds received, net of direct                           工具以扣減直接發
                    issue costs.                                                               行成本後所收取的
                                                                                               所得款項記賬。


           (vi)     Derecognition                                                       (vi)   終止確認

                    The Group derecognises a financial asset when                              當與金融資產有關
                    the contractual rights to the future cash flows                            之未來現金流合約
                    in relation to the financial asset expire or when                          權利屆滿時，或當
                    the financial asset has been transferred and the                           金融資產已轉讓，
                    transfer meets the criteria for derecognition in                           且該轉讓按照香港
                    accordance with HKFRS 9.                                                   財務報告準則第9號
                                                                                               符合終止確認標準
                                                                                               時，本集團會終止
                                                                                               確認金融資產。


                    Financial liabilities are derecognises when the                            金融負債於相關合
                    obligation specified in the relevant contract is                           約內訂明之責任解
                    discharged, cancelled or expired.                                          除、註銷或屆滿時
                                                                                               終止確認。

                     realisable value represents the estimated selling price                本。成本使用加權平均法

                     to make the sale.                                                      減去完成估計成本及進行
                                                                                            銷售所需的估計成本。

           of economic benefits is remote. Possible obligations,                   極低，否則本集團會將該
           the existence of which will only be confirmed by the                    義務披露為或然負債。須
           occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more future                      視乎某宗或多宗未來事件

           unless the probability of outflow of economic benefits is               否之潛在責任，除非經濟

      22. TRADE AND OTHER RECEIVABLES                                            22. 應收賬款及其他應收款項
             (Continued)                                                           （續）

             The Group measures the loss allowance for trade receivables            本集團計量的應收賬款虧損撥備等
             at an amount equal to lifetime ECLs. The ECLs on trade                 於全期預期信貸虧損。應收賬款之
             receivables are estimated using an individual basis by                 預期信貸虧損乃經參考債務人過往
             reference to past default experience and creditworthiness of           違約記錄及信譽以及對債務人現時
             the debtor and an analysis of the debtor’s current financial           財務狀況的分析後採用個別基準估
             position, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,       計，並就債務人的特定因素、債務
             general economic conditions of the industry in which the               人營運所處行業的整體經濟情況以
             debtors operate and an assessment of both the current as               及對報告日期現時和預測狀況方向
             well as the forecast direction of conditions at the reporting          的評估予以調整。
             date.


             For other receivables, the Group measures the loss allowance           就其他應收款項而言，本集團計量
             equal to 12-month ECLs, unless when there is a significant             與12個月預期信貸虧損等額的虧損
             increase in credit risk since initial recognition, the Group           撥備，除非信貸風險自首次確認以
             recognises lifetime ECLs. The assessment of whether lifetime           來顯著增加，則本集團會確認全期
             ECLs should be recognised is based on significant increase             預期信貸虧損。評估是否應確認全
             in the likelihood or risk of a default occurring since initial         期預期信貸虧損，乃基於自首次確
             recognition.                                                           認起出現違約的可能性或風險是否
                                                                                    顯著增加。


             There has been no change in the estimation techniques or               於報告期內所作估值技術或重大假
             significant assumptions made during the reporting period.              設並無任何變動。


             The Group’s trade and other receivables that are denominated           本集團以相關集團實體功能貨幣以
             in currencies other than the functional currencies of the              外之貨幣計值之應收賬款及其他應
             relevant group entities are set out below:                             收款項如下：

40. FINANCIAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                           40. 財務風險管理
   The main risks arising from the Group’s financial instruments          在本集團日常業務過程中，本集團
   in the normal course of the Group’s business are credit risk,          來自金融工具的主要風險為信貸風
   liquidity risk, interest rate risk and currency risk.                  險、流動資金風險、利率風險及貨
                                                                          幣風險。


   (a)     Credit risk                                                    (a)    信貸風險

           The Group’s maximum exposure to credit risk which                     因交易方未能履行責任而
           will cause a financial loss to the Group due to failure to            令本集團蒙受財務虧損之
           discharge an obligation by the counterparties is arising              最高信貸風險乃來自綜合
           from the carrying amount of the respective recognised                 財務狀況表所載列相關已
           financial assets as stated in the consolidated statement              確認金融資產之賬面值。
           of financial position.


           In order to minimise the credit risk, the management of               為將信貸風險降至最低，
           the Group has delegated a team responsible to ensure                  本集團管理層已委派一支
           that follow-up action is taken to recover overdue debts.              團隊負責確保跟進收回逾
           In addition, the Group reviews the recoverability of                  期債項之情況。此外，本
           each individual trade debt at the end of each reporting               集團會於各報告期末檢討
           period to ensure that adequate impairment losses are                  各個別應收賬款之可收回
           made for irrecoverable amounts.                                       性，確保就不可收回金額
                                                                                 作出足夠減值虧損。


           For trade receivables, the Group has applied the                      就應收賬款而言，本集團
           simplified approach in HKFRS 9 to measure the loss                    已應用香港財務報告準則
           allowance at lifetime ECLs. The Group determines                      第9號的簡化方法以按全期
           the ECLs for customer with significant balances                       預期信貸虧損計量虧損撥
           by using an individual basis, estimated based on                      備。本集團對具重大結存
           historical credit loss experience, as well as the general             客戶按個別基礎估算，以
           economic conditions of the industry in which the                      債務人過往信貸虧損經驗
           debtors operate. In this regard, the directors of the                 及債務人經營行業的整體
           Company consider that the Group has a low historical                  經濟狀況釐定預期信貸虧
           credit loss experience and there was no significant and               損。就此而言，本公司的
           adverse changes in the general economic conditions                    董事認為本集團歷史信貸
           of the industry in what the debtors operate, thus the                 虧損為低，由於債務人經
           management considered the credit risk is not material.                營的行業之總體經濟狀況
                                                                                 並無重大不利變動，因此
                                                                                 管理層認為信貸風險不重
                                                                                 大。

      41. CAPITAL RISK MANAGEMENT                                                  41. 資本風險管理
             The Group manages its capital to ensure that entities in the            本集團管理其資本，以確保本集團
             Group will be able to continue as a going concern while                 旗下實體能夠持續經營業務，並透
             maximising the return to shareholders through the optimisation          過更完善平衡債務及權益，以為股
             of the debt and equity balance. The Group’s overall strategy            東帶來最大回報。本集團整體策略
             remains unchanged from prior year.                                      與去年維持不變。


             The capital structure of the Group consists of net debt,                本集團之資本結構包括淨負債（包
             which includes loans from a fellow subsidiary and loans                 括應收一間同系附屬公司貸款及來
             from an intermediate and immediate holding company, bank                自一間中間及直接控股公司之貸款
             borrowings, net of cash and cash equivalents and equity                 及銀行借款），扣除現金及現金等
             attributable to owners of the Company, comprising issued                值項目以及本公司擁有人應佔權益
             share capital and reserves. The directors of the Company                （當中包括已發行股本及儲備）。本
             review the capital structure on a regular basis. As a part of           公司董事定期檢討資本結構。作為
             this review, the directors of the Company consider the cost             檢討其中一環，本公司董事考慮資
             of capital and the risks associated with each class of capital,         本成本及與各類資本相關的風險，
             and take appropriate actions to adjust the Group’s capital              並就此採取適當行動調整本集團資
             structure.                                                              本結構。


             Consistent with others in the industry, the Group monitors              與業內其他公司做法一致，本集團
             capital on the basis of the gearing ratio. This ratio is calculated     以資本負債比率監察資本。該比率
             as net debt divided by total capital. Net debt is calculated as         以淨負債除以資本總額計算。淨負
             total borrowings less cash and cash equivalents. Total capital is       債按借貸總額減現金及現金等值項
             calculated as equity as shown in the consolidated statement             目計算。資本總額按綜合財務狀況
             of financial position plus net debt.                                    表所列的權益加上淨負債計算。


             The gearing ratios at 31 December 2023 and 2022 were as                 於2023年及2022年12月31日的資
             follows:                                                                本負債比率如下：


                                                                                            2023       2022

                                        Partnership)，為於哈薩克斯坦成立之有限責任合夥企業，為本公司
                                        合營企業，本公司通過全資附屬公司持有其49%權益。

“SFO”                                   the Securities and Futures Ordinance (Chapter 571 of the Laws of
                                        Hong Kong) as amended from time to time.
「證券及期貨條例」                           指   不時經修訂的《證券及期貨條例》               （香港法例第571章）。

“share(s)”                              ordinary share(s) in the Company with a nominal value of HK$0.01
                                        each.
「股份」                                指   本公司每股面值0.01港元之普通股。

       “U3O8”                                                 Triuranium octoxide, a compound of uranium present as an olive
                                                              green to black, odorless solid. It is one of the more popular forms of
                                                              yellowcake and is shipped between mills and refineries in this form.
      「U3O8」                                        指         八氧化三鈾，一種鈾的化合物，呈橄欖綠至黑色，無味的固體，為
                                                              更受歡迎的黃餅形式之一，並以該形式在工廠及煉油廠之間運輸。
